.

Appendix C
Proof of Theorem
1

In this section, we provide a proof for Theorem
1
. First, suppose the corruption fraction
\epsilon=0
. In this case, under the conditions in Theorem
1
, it is well known
(
Tsitsiklis and Van Roy,, 1997
)
that
TD
(0) converges with probability one to
\theta^{*}=-\bar{A}^{-1}\bar{b}
, where
\bar{A}=\Phi^{\top}D\left(\gamma P_{\mu}-I\right)\Phi
, and
\bar{b}=\Phi^{\top}DR_{\mu}
. Notice that the rewards only affect the term
\bar{b}
. Now under the Huber-contaminated reward model described in Section
3
, for each state
s\in\mathcal{S}
, whenever in
s
, with probability
(1-\epsilon)
, the learner observes the true mean reward
R_{\mu}(s)
, and with probability
\epsilon
, a bounded, deterministic signal
C(s).
Thus, effectively, the mean of the observed reward random variable
\tilde{r}(s)
in state
s
is
\tilde{R}(s)\triangleq(1-\epsilon){R}_{\mu}(s)+\epsilon C(s).
Stacking up the individual components
\tilde{R}(s)
into a perturbed reward vector
\tilde{R}
, let us define
\tilde{b}\triangleq\Phi^{\top}D\tilde{R}.
Since everything else remains the same,
TD
(0) will now converge with probability one to

\displaystyle\tilde{\theta}^{*}=-\bar{A}^{-1}\tilde{b}=-\bar{A}^{-1}\Phi^{\top}D\tilde{R}

(13)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{(1-\epsilon)(-\bar{A}^{-1}\Phi^{\top}DR_{\mu})}_{(1-\epsilon)\theta^{*}}+\epsilon(-\bar{A}^{-1}\Phi^{\top}DC),

where we used the expression for
\theta^{*}
. This establishes the first part of the theorem.

For the second part, suppose the adversary wishes
\tilde{\theta}^{*}
to be some point
w\in\mathbb{R}^{K}
. We will show that this is possible by explicitly designing an appropriate corrupted reward vector
C_{w}
. In particular, let

C_{w}=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{\epsilon}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{\epsilon}}}D^{-1}\Phi\left(\Phi^{\top}\Phi\right)^{-1}\bar{A}\left((1-\epsilon)\theta^{*}-w\right).

We note here that in light of Assumption
1
,
\pi(s)>0,\forall s\in\mathcal{S}.
Hence,
D^{-1}
exists. Moreover, the fact that
\Phi
is full column rank ensures the existence of
\left(\Phi^{\top}\Phi\right)^{-1}
. Plugging in our choice of
C_{w}
into Eq. (
13
), it is easy to see that
\tilde{\theta}^{*}=w.
This completes the proof.

Appendix D
Performance Analysis for
RUMEM
: Proof of Theorem
2

In this section, we will prove Theorem
2
. Let us recall the setting quickly. First,
X_{1},\ldots,X_{N}
is a sequence of
N
samples drawn from a stationary Markov chain with stationary distribution
\pi
. Thus, each
X_{i}
is distributed as per
\pi
. Next, for each
i\in[N]
, with probability
(1-\epsilon)
, the learner observes a random variable
f(X_{i})
with mean
F(X_{i})
and variance at most
\rho^{2}
; and with probability
\epsilon
, an arbitrary object chosen by the adversary. The resulting sample is denoted
\tilde{f}_{i}
.

Our analysis will rely on a coupling argument that is inspired by the recent work of
Dorfman and Levy, (2022)
. To apply this argument, consider the sub-sampled sequence
X_{1},X_{\tau+1},\ldots,X_{(n-1)\tau+1},
where
n
and
\tau
are as in Section
4
. We couple this sequence with its i.i.d. counterpart
(X_{\texttt{I},1},X_{\texttt{I},\tau+1},\ldots,X_{\texttt{I},(n-1)\tau+1})\sim\pi^{\otimes n}
. Here, and henceforth throughout the proof, we will use the subscript
I
to denote the i.i.d. counterpart of a Markov sample. The next result bounds the probability of the Markovian sub-sampled sequence being different from its i.i.d. counterpart.

Lemma 5
.

(
Nagaraj et al.,, 2020
, Lemma 3)

Let
\mathcal{E}_{1}
be an event where
(X_{1},X_{\tau+1},\ldots,X_{(n-1)\tau+1})=(X_{\texttt{I},1},X_{\texttt{I},\tau+1},\ldots,X_{\texttt{I},(n-1)\tau+1}).
Then,

\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c}_{1})\leq(n-1)d_{mix}(\tau),

where
d_{mix}(\cdot)
is as defined as

d_{mix}(t)\triangleq\sup_{X\in\mathcal{X}}D_{TV}\left(\mathbb{P}(X_{t+1}\in\cdot|X_{1}=X),\pi\right).

We will call upon the above lemma at a later point in our analysis. For now, we split the proof into multiple steps.

Step 1. Bounding the number of corrupted samples.
The size of the sub-sampled set used in the
RUMEM
algorithm is
n
. Our first step is to control the number of corrupted samples in this sub-sampled set. To that end, consider an event
\mathcal{E}_{2}
, where the maximum number of corrupted samples in the sub-sampled set is
\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3\epsilon^\prime ; recall here that

\epsilon^{\prime}=\epsilon+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise where in the last step, we used
\epsilon^{\prime}>\epsilon.
Invoking Bernstein’s inequality (Lemma
4
) then yields

\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{c}\right)\leq 2e^{-\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{32}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{32}}}}.

(15)

This completes step 1.

Step 2: Statistics of each non-contaminated sample.
Suppose a particular sample
i
is non-contaminated, i.e.,
\tilde{f}_{i}=f(X_{i}).
We then have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{i}\sim\pi,f(X_{i})\sim\mathcal{D}(\cdot|X_{i})}[f(X_{i})]

(16)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{f(X_{i})\sim\mathcal{D}(\cdot|X_{i})}[f(X_{i})|X_{i}=x]\pi(x)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{f(x)\sim\mathcal{D}(\cdot|x)}[f(x)]\pi(x)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{x\in\mathcal{X}}F(x)\pi(x)

\displaystyle=\bar{f}.

Now given that
|F(x)|\leq\psi,\forall x\in\mathcal{X}
, we have
|\bar{f}|\leq\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{x\in\mathcal{X}}|F(x)|\pi(x)\leq\psi.
Using this, we can bound the variance of a non-contaminated sample as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{i}\sim\pi,f(X_{i})\sim\mathcal{D}(\cdot|X_{i})}[f^{2}(X_{i})]

(17)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{f(X_{i})\sim\mathcal{D}(\cdot|X_{i})}[f^{2}(X_{i})|X_{i}=x]\pi(x)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{f(x)\sim\mathcal{D}(\cdot|x)}[f^{2}(x)]\pi(x)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{x\in\mathcal{X}}(\bar{f}^{2}+\rho^{2})\pi(x)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{x\in\mathcal{X}}(\psi^{2}+\rho^{2})\pi(x)

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\sigma}^{2},

where
\bar{\sigma}^{2}\triangleq 2(\max\{\psi,\rho\})^{2}.
For
(a)
, we used the basic identity:
\text{Var}(X)=\mathbb{E}(X^{2})-\mathbb{E}(X)^{2}
; and for
(b)
, we used the fact that
\bar{f}\leq\psi
and that the variance of
f(x)
is at most
\rho^{2}
.

In light of Lemma
5
, we will first focus on assessing the performance of
RUMEM
on the i.i.d. sub-sampled sequence

(X_{\texttt{I},1},X_{\texttt{I},\tau+1},\ldots,X_{\texttt{I},(n-1)\tau+1})
. For this data set, let the mean of the
i
-th bucket
\mathcal{B}_{i}
be denoted
\hat{\mu}_{\texttt{I},i}
, and the final
MoM
estimate be denoted
\hat{\mu}_{\texttt{I}}=\texttt{Median}\{\hat{\mu}_{\texttt{I},1},\hat{\mu}_{\texttt{I},2},\ldots,\hat{\mu}_{\texttt{I},L}\}
.

Step 3. Bound on performance for each non-contaminated bucket under i.i.d. data.
Consider a bucket
\mathcal{B}_{i}
that only contains non-contaminated i.i.d. samples. Based on Step 2, each sample has mean
\bar{f}
and variance at most
\bar{\sigma}^{2}.

M=\lfloor n/L\rfloor
to denote the number of samples in each bucket, we obtain the following bound using Chebyshev’s inequality
\forall d>0

and
L\geq 12\epsilon^{\prime}n
. At this stage, one might be tempted to use a Hoeffding bound to control
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F})
. However, care needs to be taken here since
\tilde{N}
is random. As such, some more work is needed before one can apply Hoeffding’s inequality. We proceed by using the law of total probability to obtain:

\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F})=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2})+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}^{c})\leq\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2})+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{c}).

(22)

L\geq 12\epsilon^{\prime}n

\tilde{N}

\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathbb{P}\left(\hat{\mu}\geq\bar{f}+\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{d \bar{\sigma}}{\sqrt{\frac{N}{\tau}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{d \bar{\sigma}}{\sqrt{\frac{N}{\tau}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{d \bar{\sigma}}{\sqrt{\frac{N}{\tau}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{d \bar{\sigma}}{\sqrt{\frac{N}{\tau}}}}\right)&\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\Bigl\{\hat{\mu}\geq\bar{f}+\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{d \bar{\sigma}}{\sqrt{\frac{N}{\tau}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{d \bar{\sigma}}{\sqrt{\frac{N}{\tau}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{d \bar{\sigma}}{\sqrt{\frac{N}{\tau}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{d \bar{\sigma}}{\sqrt{\frac{N}{\tau}}}}\Bigr\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{1}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{c}\right)\\

&\overset{(b)}{\leq}\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{L}{8}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{L}{8}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{L}{8}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{L}{8}}e^{-\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{7L}{256}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{7L}{256}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{7L}{256}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{7L}{256}}}+4e^{-\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{32}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{32}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{32}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3\epsilon^\prime n}{32}}}+(n-1)d_{mix}(\tau).\end{aligned}$}}}

(27)

For (a), we used Eq. (
26
), and for (b), we invoked Lemma
5
. Our goal is to now ensure that each term appearing on the R.H.S. of the above display is bounded from above by
\delta/6
. Let us start with the term
(n-1)d_{mix}(\tau)
. From
Dorfman and Levy, (2022)
, we know that for any positive integer
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
, if
\tau=\ell\tau_{mix}
, then
d_{mix}(\tau)\leq 2^{-\ell}
. Thus, picking
\tau=\lceil\log_{2}\left(6N/\delta\right)\tau_{mix}\rceil
, we obtain

(n-1)d_{mix}(\tau)\leq N\cdot 2^{-\log_{2}\left(6N/\delta\right)}\leq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{6}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{6}}}.

Next, given our choice of
\epsilon^{\prime}=\epsilon+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{32}{3n}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{32}{3n}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{32}{3n}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{32}{3n}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{32}{3n}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{32}{3n}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{32}{3n}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{32}{3n}}$}}{\raise Appendix E
Main Convergence Analysis for
Robust-TD
: Proof of Theorem
3

In this section, we will prove our main convergence result for
Robust-TD
, namely Theorem
3
. The key new technical ingredient that we need in our analysis is a robust estimate of the object
\bar{b}
that features in the mean-path TD update direction
\bar{g}(\theta)=\bar{A}\theta+\bar{b}
. This is achieved in the following lemma.

Lemma 6
.

(
Adversarial Perturbation Bound
)
Suppose Assumption
1
holds, and the initial distribution of
s_{0}
is the steady-state distribution
\pi.
There exists a universal constant
c>0
, such that if the burn-in time
\bar{T}
satisfies the requirement in Theorem
3
,
then the following is true for all
t\geq\bar{T}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}]\leq c\left(\epsilon+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}}\right)K\sigma^{2}_{1},

(30)

\displaystyle\textrm{where}\hskip 5.69054pt\sigma_{1}=\max\{1,\bar{r},\rho\}.

Proof.

The proof comprises three steps. In the first step, we use the guarantees from the
RUMEM
estimator in Theorem
2
to establish a high-probability bound on the error
\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|
. In the second step, we use the result from step 1 to argue that with high probability, the resetting operating in line 8 of Algorithm
1
gets bypassed, and
\hat{b}_{t}
corresponds to the output of the robust estimation procedure in line 6 of Algorithm
1
. Finally, in the last step, we establish a mean-square error bound by leveraging the thresholding operation in line 8 of
Robust-TD
. We now proceed to provide the details for each of these steps.

Step 1: A high-probability estimate on
\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|
.
Let us start by fixing a component
i\in[K]
of
\bar{b}
and
\hat{b}_{t}
, and a time-step
t\geq\bar{T}
. Now consider the estimation process in line 6 of Algorithm
1
:
[\hat{b}_{t}]_{i}=\textrm{{RUMEM}}\left(\{y_{i,k}\}_{0\leq k\leq t};\delta=1/(KT^{2})\right)
, and
y_{i,k}=[\phi(s_{k})]_{i}\tilde{r}_{k}
. We wish to relate this estimation step to the robust mean estimation set up in Section
4
. To that end, let us make the following observations by considering the data set
\mathcal{S}=\{y_{i,k}\}_{0\leq k\leq t}
. First, note that since the initial distribution of
s_{0}
is the stationary distribution
\pi
, the resulting Markov chain
\{s_{0},s_{1},\ldots\}
induced by the policy
\mu
is stationary. Thus,
s_{t}\sim\pi,\forall t.
Next, let us consider the statistics of a sample
y_{i,k}
that is not corrupted, i.e., a sample for which
\tilde{r}_{k}=r(s_{k})\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mu}(\cdot|s_{k})
. For such a sample, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s_{k}\sim\pi,r(s_{k})\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mu}(\cdot|s_{k})}[y_{i,k}]

(31)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\mathbb{E}_{r(s_{k})\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mu}(\cdot|s_{k})}[[\phi(s_{k})]_{i}r(s_{k})|s_{k}=s]\pi(s)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}[\phi(s)]_{i}\mathbb{E}_{{r(s)\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mu}(\cdot|s)}}[r(s)]\pi(s)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}[\phi(s)]_{i}R_{\mu}(s)\pi(s)

\displaystyle=[\bar{b}]_{i}.

In other words, the mean of an uncorrupted sample corresponds exactly to the
i
-th component of
\bar{b}
. Proceeding as above, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s_{k}\sim\pi,r(s_{k})\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mu}(\cdot|s_{k})}[y^{2}_{i,k}]

(32)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\mathbb{E}_{r(s_{k})\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mu}(\cdot|s_{k})}[([\phi(s_{k})]_{i}r(s_{k}))^{2}|s_{k}=s]\pi(s)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}([\phi(s)]_{i})^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{r(s)\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mu}(\cdot|s)}}[r^{2}(s)]\pi(s)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\mathbb{E}_{{r(s)\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mu}(\cdot|s)}}[r^{2}(s)]\pi(s)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}({R}^{2}_{\mu}(s)+\rho^{2})\pi(s)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}(\bar{r}^{2}+\rho^{2})\pi(s)

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}2\sigma^{2}_{1}.

In the above steps, for (a), we used the fact that
\|\phi(s)\|^{2}\leq 1,\forall s\in\mathcal{S}.
For (b), we used that the variance of the random variable
r(s)
is upper-bounded by
\rho^{2}
. To arrive at (c), we used the uniform upper bound on the means of the rewards:
|R_{\mu}(s)|\leq\bar{r},\forall s\in\mathcal{S}.
Finally, for (d), we used
\sigma_{1}=\max\{1,\bar{r},\rho\}.
We conclude that for each sample in the data set
\mathcal{S}
, with probability
1-\epsilon,
we observe a “clean" random variable with mean
[\bar{b}]_{i}
, and variance at most
2\sigma^{2}_{1}.

Given that the
RUMEM
sub-routine is invoked in line 6 of Algorithm
1
with
\delta=1/(KT^{2})
, and number of samples
N=(t+1)
, we have from Theorem
2
that with probability at least
1-1/(KT^{2})
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert[\hat{b}_{t}]_{i}-[\bar{b}]_{i}\right\rvert\leq C\sigma_{1}\left(\sqrt{\epsilon}+\sqrt{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau}{N}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau}{N}}}\log\left(\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{N}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{N}{\delta}}}\right)}\right)

(33)

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma_{1}\left(\sqrt{\epsilon}+\sqrt{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2 T^2\right)}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2 \tau_{mix} \log_2(6NKT^2)\log\left(N K T^2\right)}{t}}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma_{1}\left(\sqrt{\epsilon}+2\log(12KT^{3})\sqrt{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}}}\right),

where we used the expression for
\tau
in Eq. (
5
), and the fact that
N=t+1\leq 2T
. Union-bounding over each component
i\in[K]
, and over all time-steps
t\geq\bar{T}
, we have that with probability at least
1-1/T
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert[\hat{b}_{t}]_{i}-[\bar{b}]_{i}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma_{1}\left(\sqrt{\epsilon}+2\log(12KT^{3})\sqrt{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}}}\right),

(34)

\displaystyle\forall i\in[K],\forall t\geq\bar{T}.

Let us call the event on which the above inequalities hold
\mathcal{E}
. It then follows that on the event
\mathcal{E}
, the following is true:

\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|\leq C\sqrt{K}\sigma_{1}\left(\sqrt{\epsilon}+2\log(12KT^{3})\sqrt{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}}}\right),\forall t\geq\bar{T}.

(35)

To complete step 1, we note that for us to be able to invoke Theorem
2
and arrive at the bound in Eq. (
33
), we need the number of samples in
\mathcal{S}
, namely
N=t+1
, to satisfy the requirement
N\geq 4L\tau
. Here, recall from the description of
RUMEM
that
L
is the number of buckets, and
\tau
is the sub-sampling gap. Using the expressions for
\tau
and
L
in Eq. (
5
), along with
N=t+1\leq 2T
and
\delta=1/(KT^{2})
, we have that

4L\tau\leq 96\epsilon N+c^{\prime}\tau_{mix}\log^{2}(KT),

where
c^{\prime}
is some suitably large universal constant. So the requirement that
N\geq 4L\tau
is met if
\epsilon\leq 1/(192)
and
t\geq 2c^{\prime}\tau_{mix}\log^{2}(KT)
- the latter requirement is taken care of by the choice of the burn-in time
\bar{T}
in Theorem
3
. This concludes step 1.

Step 2.
Next, we claim that on the good event
\mathcal{E}
, line 8 of Algorithm
1
will always get bypassed, and
\hat{b}_{t}
will be the output of the estimation scheme in line 6 of Algorithm
1
. To see this, we start by noting that

\bar{b}=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\phi(s)R_{\mu}(s)\pi(s).

Thus,

\displaystyle\|\bar{b}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\|\phi(s)\||R_{\mu}(s)|\pi(s)

(36)

\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\bar{r}\pi(s)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{1},

where for second inequality, we used
\|\phi(s)\|\leq 1,\forall s\in\mathcal{S}
, and for the third inequality, we used
|R_{\mu}(s)|\leq\bar{r}\leq\sigma_{1}.
Combining the above observation with Eq. (
35
), we conclude that on the event
\mathcal{E}
, the following is true:

\|\hat{b}_{t}\|\leq\underbrace{C\sqrt{K}\sigma_{1}\left(\sqrt{\epsilon}+2\log(12KT^{3})\sqrt{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\tau_{mix}}{t}}}}\right)}_{G_{t}}+\sigma_{1},\forall t\geq\bar{T}.

This immediately leads to the claim that line 8 of Algorithm
1
always gets bypassed on event
\mathcal{E}.

Step 3. Bound on the expected value of
\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}
.
Let us start by noting that if
\|\hat{b}_{t}\|>G_{t}+\sigma_{1}
, then as per the thresholding operation in line 8 of Algorithm
1
,
\hat{b}_{t}
gets reset to
0
. In this case,
\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|=\|\bar{b}\|\leq\sigma_{1}
, where in the last step, we used Eq. (
36
). We conclude that thanks to the thresholding operation, the following is always true deterministically:

\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|\leq G_{t}+2\sigma_{1},\forall t\geq\bar{T}.

(37)

Furthermore, from the requirement on
\bar{T}
in Theorem
3
, we have that
\bar{T}\geq\tau_{mix}(2\log(12KT^{3})^{2}.
This tells us that for
t\geq\bar{T},G_{t}\leq 2\sqrt{K}C\sigma_{1}.
We then have that

\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|\leq G_{t}+2\sigma_{1}\leq 2(\sqrt{K}C+1)\sigma_{1},\forall t\geq\bar{T}.

(38)

We are now in a position to bound
\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}]
. Using
\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{V}}
as an indicator for any event
\mathcal{V}
, we have
\forall t\geq\bar{T}
:

\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}]&=\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}}]+\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}}]\\
&\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}}]+\left(2(\sqrt{K}C+1)\sigma_{1}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}}]\\
&=\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}}]+\left(2(\sqrt{K}C+1)\sigma_{1}\right)^{2}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\\
&\overset{(b)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}}]+\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}\\

&\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}}]+\left(2(\sqrt{K}C+1)\sigma_{1}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}}]\\
&=\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}}]+\left(2(\sqrt{K}C+1)\sigma_{1}\right)^{2}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\\
&\overset{(b)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}}]+\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}\\

&\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}}]+\left(2(\sqrt{K}C+1)\sigma_{1}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}}]\\

&\overset{(b)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}[\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{E}}]+\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{(\sqrt{K}C +1)^2 4\sigma^2_1}{T}}\\
&\overset{(c)}{\leq}O(\sigma_{1}^{2}K\epsilon)+O\left(\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(12 K T^3) \sigma^2_1 K \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(12 K T^3) \sigma^2_1 K \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(12 K T^3) \sigma^2_1 K \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(12 K T^3) \sigma^2_1 K \tau_{mix}}{t}}\right)+\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\sigma^2_1 K}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\sigma^2_1 K}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\sigma^2_1 K}{T}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\sigma^2_1 K}{T}}\\
&\leq O\left(\left(\epsilon+\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}\right)K\sigma^{2}_{1}\right).\end{aligned}$}}}

(39)

In the above steps, for (a), we used Eq. (
38
) to bound
\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|
on the event
\mathcal{E}^{c}
. For (b), we used
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\leq 1/T
. Finally, in view of steps 1 and 2, we used Eq. (
35
) to bound
\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|
on the event
\mathcal{E}.
This concludes the proof.
∎

Equipped with the above lemma, our next step is establishing a one-step mean-square error decomposition. Before we do so, we remind the reader here of some notation: recall that
d_{t}=\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}
,
\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta)=A_{t}\theta+\hat{b}_{t},
and
\sigma=\max\{\sigma_{1},\|\theta^{*}\|,\|\theta_{0}\|\}.
Given the guarantee from Lemma
6
, we will also find it useful to employ the following notation:

B_{t}=c\left(\epsilon+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}$}}{\raise \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\log^2(KT) \tau_{mix}}{t}}}\right)K\sigma^{2}_{1}.

(40)

We have the following result.

Lemma 7
.

(
Main Recursion
)

Suppose the conditions in the statement of Theorem
3
are met. Then, the following is true
\forall t\geq\bar{T}
:

\mathbb{E}[d_{t+1}]\leq(1-\alpha\beta+12\alpha^{2})\mathbb{E}[d_{t}]+O(\alpha^{2}\sigma^{2}K)+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}}+\mathbb{E}[M_{t}],

(41)

where
\beta=\omega(1-\gamma)
,
M_{t}=2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},(A_{t}-\bar{A})\theta_{t}\rangle,
and
B_{t}
is as in Eq. (
40
).

Proof.

From the update rule of
Robust-TD
in line 10 of Algorithm
1
, we have:

\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}&=\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\rangle+\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}\\
&=\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle\\
&+\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})-\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle\\
&=\underbrace{\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle}_{(*)}+\underbrace{\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}}_{(**)}\\
&\hskip 5.69054pt+\underbrace{2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},(A_{t}-\bar{A})\theta_{t}\rangle}_{(***)}+\underbrace{2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\rangle}_{(****)}.\end{aligned}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}&=\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\rangle+\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}\\
&=\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle\\
&+\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})-\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle\\
&=\underbrace{\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle}_{(*)}+\underbrace{\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}}_{(**)}\\
&\hskip 5.69054pt+\underbrace{2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},(A_{t}-\bar{A})\theta_{t}\rangle}_{(***)}+\underbrace{2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\rangle}_{(****)}.\end{aligned}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\begin{aligned} \|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}&=\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\rangle+\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}\\
&=\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle\\
&+\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})-\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle\\
&=\underbrace{\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle}_{(*)}+\underbrace{\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}}_{(**)}\\
&\hskip 5.69054pt+\underbrace{2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},(A_{t}-\bar{A})\theta_{t}\rangle}_{(***)}+\underbrace{2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\rangle}_{(****)}.\end{aligned}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\begin{aligned} \|\theta_{t+1}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}&=\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\rangle+\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}\\
&=\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle\\
&+\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})-\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle\\
&=\underbrace{\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\bar{g}(\theta_{t})\rangle}_{(*)}+\underbrace{\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|^{2}}_{(**)}\\
&\hskip 5.69054pt+\underbrace{2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},(A_{t}-\bar{A})\theta_{t}\rangle}_{(***)}+\underbrace{2\alpha\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\rangle}_{(****)}.\end{aligned}$}}}

(42)

Before proceeding further, it is instructive to take a moment to interpret each of the terms in the above decomposition. The term
(*)
captures the “steady-state" behavior of
TD
(0) and is responsible for driving the iterates toward
\theta^{*}
. This term can be analyzed using Lemma
2
, yielding:

(*)\leq(1-2\alpha\beta)d_{t}.

(43)

Each of the remaining three terms can be viewed as a perturbation/disturbance to the nominal/steady-state dynamics. As for
(**)
and
(****)
, since they feature
\hat{b}_{t}
, which is processed based on contaminated rewards, these terms capture the effects of adversarial perturbations. On the other hand, the term
(***)
depends on the “closeness" between
A_{t}
and its steady-state version
\bar{A}
, which, in turn, is dictated by how quickly the underlying Markov chain is mixing. In the remainder of the proof, we bound the terms that contain adversarial effects. We start by noting that thanks to the thresholding operation, we have that
\|\hat{b}_{t}\|\leq G_{t}+\sigma_{1}\leq G_{t}+\sigma,\forall t\geq\bar{T}.
Furthermore, in the analysis of Lemma
6
, we argued that for all
t\geq\bar{T}
, it holds that
G_{t}\leq 2\sqrt{K}C\sigma_{1}\leq 2\sqrt{K}C\sigma,
where
C
is some universal constant. Thus, we have

\|\hat{b}_{t}\|\leq(2\sqrt{K}C+1)\sigma=O(\sqrt{K}\sigma),\forall t\geq\bar{T}.

(44)

To see how the above bound helps us, observe:

\displaystyle(**)

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\|A_{t}\theta_{t}+\hat{b}_{t}\|^{2}

(45)

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\|A_{t}(\theta_{t}-\theta^{*})+A_{t}\theta^{*}+\hat{b}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\alpha^{2}\|A_{t}\|^{2}d_{t}+3\alpha^{2}\|A_{t}\|^{2}\|\theta^{*}\|^{2}+3\alpha^{2}\|\hat{b}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 12\alpha^{2}d_{t}+12\alpha^{2}\sigma^{2}+3\alpha^{2}\|\hat{b}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 12\alpha^{2}d_{t}+O(\alpha^{2}K\sigma^{2}),

where we used
\|A_{t}\|\leq 2
and
\|\theta^{*}\|\leq\sigma.
Now for the term
(****)
, we have

\displaystyle(****)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\beta\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|^{2}+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}}\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}

(46)

\displaystyle=\alpha\beta d_{t}+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}}\|\hat{b}_{t}-\bar{b}\|^{2}.

Taking expectations on both sides of the above inequality, and using Lemma
6
, we have

\mathbb{E}[(****)]\leq\alpha\beta\mathbb{E}[d_{t}]+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha B_t}{\beta}}}.

Taking expectations on both sides of Eq. (
42
), and then combining the bounds on
(*),(**),
and
(****)
from Eq. (
43
), (
45
), and the above display, respectively, leads to the claim of the lemma.
∎

From Lemma
7
, it is clear that in order to proceed further, we need to bound the term
\mathbb{E}[M_{t}]
that corresponds to the bias introduced by Markov noise. To that end, we will require an intermediate result. Before stating this result, we remind the reader that
\tau^{\prime}=\tau^{\prime}_{mix}(\alpha)
is as defined in Section
6
.

Lemma 8
.

(
Bounding the Drift
)

Suppose the conditions in the statement of Theorem
3
are met. Then, the following bound holds
\forall t\geq\bar{T}+\tau^{\prime}
:

\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|^{2}\leq O(\alpha^{2}\tau^{\prime 2})d_{t}+O(\alpha^{2}\tau^{\prime 2}K\sigma^{2}).

(47)

Proof.

From Eq. (
44
), recall that
\|\hat{b}_{t}\|\leq O(\sqrt{K}\sigma),\forall t\geq\bar{T}.
Using this, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\theta_{t+1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\theta_{t}\|+\alpha\|\tilde{g}_{t}(\theta_{t})\|

(48)

\displaystyle\leq\|\theta_{t}\|+\alpha\left(\|A_{t}\|\|\theta_{t}\|+\|\hat{b}_{t}\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1+2\alpha)\|\theta_{t}\|+O(\alpha\sqrt{K}\sigma).

Rolling out the above recursion, we obtain the following for any
k\in[t-\tau^{\prime},t]:

\|\theta_{k}\|\leq(1+2\alpha)^{\tau^{\prime}}\|\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|+O(\alpha\sqrt{K}\sigma)\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{\ell=0}^{\tau^{\prime}}(1+2\alpha)^{\ell}.

Since
(1+x)\leq\exp(x),\forall x\in\mathbb{R}
, note that
(1+2\alpha)^{\tau^{\prime}}\leq\exp(0.25)<2
, for
\alpha\leq 1/(8\tau^{\prime}).
Using this to simplify the above display, we have that for any
k\in[t-\tau^{\prime},t]
, the following is true:

\|\theta_{k}\|\leq 2\|\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma)\leq 2\|\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|+O(\sqrt{K}\sigma),

(49)

where in the last step, we used
\alpha\tau^{\prime}\leq 1.

Let us now observe the following chain of inequalities:

\displaystyle\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=t-\tau^{\prime}}^{t-1}\|\theta_{k+1}-\theta_{k}\|

(50)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=t-\tau^{\prime}}^{t-1}\|\tilde{g}_{k}(\theta_{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=t-\tau^{\prime}}^{t-1}\left(\|A_{k}\|\|\theta_{k}\|+\|\hat{b}_{k}\|\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\alpha\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=t-\tau^{\prime}}^{t-1}\left(2\|\theta_{k}\|+O(\sqrt{K}\sigma)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\alpha\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=t-\tau^{\prime}}^{t-1}\left(4\|\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|+O(\sqrt{K}\sigma)\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma)

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha\tau^{\prime}\left(\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|+\|\theta_{t}\|\right)+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|+4\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma).

In the above steps, for (a), we used
\|A_{k}\|\leq 2
and
\|\hat{b}_{k}\|\leq O(\sqrt{K}\sigma)
; for (b), we used Eq. (
49
); and for (c), we used
\alpha\tau^{\prime}\leq 1/8.
Rearranging Eq. (
50
) and simplifying, we obtain:

\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|\leq 8\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma)\leq 8\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma),$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|\leq 8\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma)\leq 8\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma),$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|\leq 8\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma)\leq 8\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma),$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|\leq 8\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma)\leq 8\alpha\tau^{\prime}\|\theta_{t}-\theta^{*}\|+O(\alpha\tau^{\prime}\sqrt{K}\sigma),$}}}

where we used
\|\theta^{*}\|\leq\sigma.
Squaring both sides of the above display leads to the claim of the lemma.
∎

With the above lemma in hand, we can now bound the term
\mathbb{E}[M_{t}]
.

Lemma 9
.

(
Markovian Bias Bound
)

Let
M_{t}
be as defined in Lemma
7
.
Suppose the conditions in the statement of Theorem
3
are met. Then, the following bound holds
\forall t\geq\bar{T}+\tau^{\prime}
:

\mathbb{E}[M_{t}]\leq O(\alpha^{2}\tau^{\prime})\mathbb{E}[d_{t}]+O(\alpha^{2}\tau^{\prime})K\sigma^{2}.

Proof.

Let us start by splitting the term
\langle\theta_{t}-\theta^{*},(A_{t}-\bar{A})\theta_{t}\rangle=T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4}
into the four parts shown below:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=\langle\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}},(A_{t}-\bar{A})\theta_{t}\rangle

(51)

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=\langle\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}-\theta^{*},(A_{t}-\bar{A})\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\rangle

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=\langle\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}-\theta^{*},A_{t}(\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}})\rangle

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle=\langle\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}-\theta^{*},\bar{A}(\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}-\theta_{t})\rangle.

In what follows, we proceed to bound each of the four terms above.

Bounding
T_{1}
.
We bound
T_{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}}\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|^{2}+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\alpha\tau'}{2}}}\|(A_{t}-\bar{A})\theta_{t}\|^{2}

(52)

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}}\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\|^{2}+\alpha\tau^{\prime}\left(\|A_{t}\|^{2}+\|\bar{A}\|^{2}\right)\|\theta_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2 \alpha\tau'}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2
